    <div class="life-dossiers" data-life-dossiers aria-live="polite">
      <article class="life-dossier is-active" id="work" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="branch-work" data-life-panel="work" tabindex="-1">
        <header class="life-dossier__header">
          <p>01 <span>/</span> Work</p>
          <span>Enterprise systems <i>/</i> 2024–now</span>
        </header>
        <h2>Make difficult work operable.</h2>
        <p class="life-dossier__lede">At Fiserv, I help turn AI-assisted delivery and application-security work across more than twenty engineering teams into one dependable operating model.</p>
        <dl class="life-evidence" aria-label="Selected delivery outcome">
          <div><dt>20+</dt><dd>engineering teams</dd></div>
          <div><dt><small>about</small> 4,000 / 200</dt><dd>findings, before / after</dd></div>
          <div><dt>2 weeks</dt><dd>coordinated execution</dd></div>
        </dl>
        <p class="life-dossier__note">In one shared two-week effort, I helped reduce a third-party-identified security backlog from about 4,000 findings to about 200. The result was not a heroic script, but a path people could review, own, and repeat.</p>
        <a class="life-text-link" href="https://www.linkedin.com/in/bcalderonmorales-cmoe/">Experience on LinkedIn <span aria-hidden="true">↗</span></a>
      </article>

      <article class="life-dossier" id="make" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="branch-make" data-life-panel="make" tabindex="-1">
        <header class="life-dossier__header">
          <p>02 <span>/</span> Make</p>
          <span>Open source <i>/</i> tools that travel</span>
        </header>
        <h2>Build tools that outlive the moment.</h2>
        <p class="life-dossier__lede">I build practical, inspectable tools for people working with coding agents, designed so the workflow can outlive a model or vendor.</p>
        <div class="life-artifacts">
          <a href="https://github.com/BA-CalderonMorales/terminal-jarvis">
            <span class="life-artifacts__index">A</span>
            <span><strong>Terminal Jarvis</strong><small>Rust / one interface / Cargo, npm, Homebrew</small></span>
            <i aria-hidden="true">↗</i>
          </a>
          <a href="https://github.com/BA-CalderonMorales/agent-harness">
            <span class="life-artifacts__index">B</span>
            <span><strong>Agent Harness</strong><small>Go / clean-room architecture / permission controls</small></span>
            <i aria-hidden="true">↗</i>
          </a>
        </div>
        <p class="life-dossier__note">Predictable beats clever. A good tool should feel more like a shovel than a magic trick.</p>
      </article>

      <article class="life-dossier" id="serve" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="branch-serve" data-life-panel="serve" tabindex="-1">
        <header class="life-dossier__header">
          <p>03 <span>/</span> Serve</p>
          <span>United States Marine Corps <i>/</i> 2014–2019</span>
        </header>
        <h2>Precision came before software.</h2>
        <p class="life-dossier__lede">As a Legal Chief and Court Reporter, confidentiality, complete records, and operational readiness were not abstractions. They were the work.</p>
        <blockquote class="life-principle">
          <p>Ownership should be visible.<br>Decisions should be traceable.<br>Automation should be reviewable.</p>
        </blockquote>
        <p class="life-dossier__note">That root system still shapes the platforms, handoffs, and guardrails I build today.</p>
      </article>

      <article class="life-dossier" id="learn" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="branch-learn" data-life-panel="learn" tabindex="-1">

        <h2>Learn in public. Leave useful notes.</h2>
        <p class="life-dossier__lede">I turn experiments into notes, cheat sheets, small tools, and maintained documentation so the next person can begin further along.</p>
        <div class="life-learning">

          <p><span>May 2027</span>Expected graduation, University of Nebraska at Omaha</p>
